- Active motifs: falsehood contrary to truth (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); attributing a person or claim to lying (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); lying self (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); disfigured or ugly fabrication (`quranic:root_000831:B004/m01`); disfigured appearance (`quranic:root_000832:B002/m01`); formed image (`quranic:root_000891:B003/m01`); false claim about what was drunk or done (`quranic:root_000783:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:18 `ص و ر` (ٱلصُّورِ); 78:24 `ش ر ب` (شَرَابًا); 78:28 `ك ذ ب` (كَذَّبُوا۟/كِذَّابًۭا); 78:29 `ش ي ء` (شَىْءٍ).
- Synthesis: Denial is productive rather than empty: it assigns falsehood, forms an alternative image, and attributes acts that did not occur. The “lying self” is the interior agent that sustains this counterfeit account.

#### Subchannel C. Failed Charge, Interrupted Milk, Stopped Animal, and Mirage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: An appearance or promised continuation begins convincingly but fails under persistence: attack falters, milk ceases, an animal stops, fabric misrepresents itself, and apparent water vanishes.
- Active motifs: charge that fails to press home (`quranic:root_001290:B004/m01`); action occurring without delay (`quranic:root_001290:B005/m01`); milk that does not continue (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); wild animal running then stopping to look back (`quranic:root_001290:B007/m01`); patterned cloth that lies by appearance (`quranic:root_001290:B009/m01`); mirage resembling water (`quranic:root_000691:B009/m01`); mottled bodily color (`quranic:root_000318:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:20 `س ر ب` (سَرَابًا); 78:27 `ح س ب` (حِسَابًۭا); 78:28 `ك ذ ب` (كَذَّبُوا۟/كِذَّابًۭا).
- Synthesis: These motifs share a strict failure signature: initial motion or appearance promises continuation, but the promise collapses when tested. Mirage is the environmental form of the same instability that appears in attack, lactation, animal movement, and textile display.

### 18. P2 (78:17-30): Transgression as Overflow and Punishment as Accumulation
- Semantic invariant: Exceeding a limit generates uncontrolled expansion, and that excess returns as a penalty that continues to increase.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:22 names the transgressors and 78:30 promises nothing but increased punishment.
- Surprising reach: Moral excess is rendered as floodwater, swollen air, elevated domination, forced display, market escalation, added body parts, and packed travel stores.

#### Subchannel A. Boundary-Crossing, Flood, and Inflated Power
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A subject crosses a governing limit, rises like floodwater, and converts expansion into domination or misleading grandeur.
- Active motifs: exceeding the limit in rebellion (`quranic:root_000937:B001/m01`); floodwater rising and sweeping away (`quranic:root_000937:B002/m01`); tyrannical head of error (`quranic:root_000937:B003/m01`); high smooth slab or summit (`quranic:root_000937:B005/m01`); boastful over-expansion (`quranic:root_000657:B003/m01`); pride inflated by air (`quranic:root_001529:B006/m01`); coercive elevation (`quranic:root_000745:B003/m01`); competitive self-elevation (`quranic:root_000745:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:18 `ن ف خ` (يُنفَخُ); 78:19 `س م و` (ٱلسَّمَآءُ); 78:22 `ط غ ي` (لِّلطَّٰغِينَ); 78:30 `ز ي د` (نَّزِيدَكُمْ).
- Synthesis: Transgression is vertical and hydraulic: it crosses a boundary, rises, and sweeps outward. Pride and domination are not separate topics but social forms of the same uncontrolled expansion.

#### Subchannel B. Addition, Growth, Bidding, and Provisioned Surplus
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Quantity grows through added components, competitive bids, productive yield, and stored travel provision.
- Active motifs: increase entering a thing (`quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`); added appendages or components (`quranic:root_000657:B002/m01`); bidding for an increment (`quranic:root_000657:B004/m01`); stored provisions (`quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`); agricultural yield arriving (`quranic:root_000009:B007/m01`); treasury opened onto contents (`quranic:root_001124:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:18 `ء ت ي` (فَتَأْتُونَ); 78:19 `ف ت ح` (فُتِحَتِ); 78:30 `ز ي د` (نَّزِيدَكُمْ).
- Synthesis: Increase can be constructive when it supplies parts, yield, and provisions. Competitive bidding and opened treasure expose the point at which accumulation becomes self-amplifying rather than need-bounded.

#### Subchannel C. Penalty That Fits and Then Intensifies
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An affliction is first matched to conduct, then repeatedly added until punishment becomes the only remaining increment.
- Active motifs: painful punishment (`quranic:root_000994:B005/m01`); affliction learned by tasting (`quranic:root_000526:B002/m01`); act answered by recompense (`quranic:root_000244:B001/m01`); amount exactly matching its counterpart (`quranic:root_001668:B004/m01`); additional increase (`quranic:root_000657:B001/m01`); calamity arriving and destroying (`quranic:root_000009:B011/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:18 `ء ت ي` (فَتَأْتُونَ); 78:26 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ), `و ف ق` (وِفَاقًا); 78:30 `ذ و ق` (فَذُوقُوا۟), `ز ي د` (نَّزِيدَكُمْ), `ع ذ ب` (عَذَابًا).
- Synthesis: Fit and increase form a sequence rather than a contradiction. The penalty corresponds to prior conduct, but persistence in excess makes punitive experience itself the field in which further increments occur.

### 19. P2 (78:17-30): Separation Producing Joints, Passages, and Textual Order
- Semantic invariant: Separation is productive when it creates a usable joint, traversable boundary, social partition, or intelligible segmentation.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:17 foregrounds separation, while 78:19 turns a formerly continuous sky into articulated doors.
- Surprising reach: The same dividing action makes anatomical joints, mountain watercourses, low walls, weaning, kin groups, necklace spacers, verse endings, and grammatical boundaries.

#### Subchannel A. Joint, Cleft, and Low Boundary
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A cut defines where two parts meet, where water passes, or where a short barrier separates adjoining spaces.
- Active motifs: distinction making a visible boundary (`quranic:root_001159:B001/m01`); joint between bones or components (`quranic:root_001159:B005/m01`); mountain cleft conducting water (`quranic:root_001159:B006/m01`); short wall below a city rampart (`quranic:root_001159:B007/m01`); hard substrate resisting the cut (`quranic:root_000217:B005/m01`); boundary gate (`quranic:root_000163:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:17 `ف ص ل` (ٱلْفَصْلِ); 78:19 `ب و ب` (أَبْوَٰبًۭا); 78:20 `ج ب ل` (ٱلْجِبَالُ).
- Synthesis: Separation does not simply pull things apart; it specifies their interface. Joint, cleft, wall, and gate are each boundaries whose usefulness depends on a controlled relation between divided sides.

#### Subchannel B. Weaning, Kin Segment, and Shared Partition
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A dependent is detached from nurture, a near kin segment is distinguished within a people, and partners divide a formerly shared holding.
- Active motifs: young animal or child separated from mother or nursing (`quranic:root_001159:B003/m01`); a man’s nearest kin segment (`quranic:root_001159:B009/m01`); partners separating or apportioning a common matter (`quranic:root_001159:B014/m01`); collateral kin and inheritance (`quranic:root_001315:B004/m01`); milk failing or ceasing (`quranic:root_001290:B006/m01`); restraint or weaning from a thing (`quranic:root_000994:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:17 `ف ص ل` (ٱلْفَصْلِ); 78:28 `ك ذ ب` (كِذَّابًۭا); 78:29 `ك ل ل` (كُلَّ); 78:30 `ع ذ ب` (عَذَابًا).
- Synthesis: Weaning supplies the bodily prototype of social partition: an attached relation is cut without erasing origin. Kin segment, inheritance, and partnership division extend that operation into collective and legal structure.

#### Subchannel C. Verse Ending, Necklace Spacer, and Grammatical Segmentation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Small separators organize a sequence of letters, verses, beads, clauses, or categories into readable units.
- Active motifs: tongue that distinguishes by articulation (`quranic:root_001159:B004/m01`); verse endings and textual divisions (`quranic:root_001159:B011/m01`); spacer bead between pearls (`quranic:root_001159:B012/m01`); explanation into meaningful parts (`quranic:root_001159:B013/m01`); grammatical separator (`quranic:root_001159:B015/m01`); letters joined into writing (`quranic:root_001283:B002/m01`); making a thing into doors or categories (`quranic:root_000163:B002/m01`); sign as an assembled unit (`quranic:root_000074:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:17 `ف ص ل` (ٱلْفَصْلِ); 78:19 `ب و ب` (أَبْوَٰبًۭا); 78:28 `ء ي ي` (بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا); 78:29 `ك ت ب` (كِتَٰبًۭا).
- Synthesis: Textual intelligibility depends on bounded units. Spacer, ending, category, and grammatical separator create intervals that keep joined material ordered rather than undifferentiated.

### 20. P3 (78:31-40): Enclosed Cultivation, Field Labor, and Irrigation
- Semantic invariant: Productive abundance arises when land is enclosed, planted, guarded, worked with tools, and supplied by controlled water.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:32 names enclosed gardens and grapes, while 78:37 places them within the Lord’s earth-and-sky domain.
- Surprising reach: The garden becomes an operating field with seed-covering farmers, plough fittings, vineyard guards, adzes, craftsmen, rain, cisterns, water skins, and night approaches to a water source.

#### Subchannel A. Bounded Orchard and Fertile Interior
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A circular or walled boundary encloses fertile ground, trees, and retained water, distinguishing a cultivated interior from surrounding land.
- Active motifs: encircling a thing (`quranic:root_000300:B001/m01`); walled tree-garden or watered meadow (`quranic:root_000300:B003/m01`); fertile productive earth (`quranic:root_000025:B002/m01`); persistent green plant (`quranic:root_000532:B012/m01`); abundant gathered water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); fruit sheath or enclosing husk (`quranic:root_001307:B010/m01`); smooth open ground outside growth (`quranic:root_000871:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ح د ق` (حَدَآئِقَ); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37 `ء ر ض` (ٱلْأَرْضِ); 78:38 `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا); 78:40 `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ).
- Synthesis: The orchard is defined by controlled enclosure: boundary, fertile substrate, plants, water, and fruit-cover all maintain a productive inside. The bare level field provides its spatial counter-condition.

#### Subchannel B. Vine, Fruit, Sapling, and Renewed Leaf
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Vines and small palms establish growth, bear fruit, leaf again, and emit the scent and appearance of ripeness.
- Active motifs: grape and grapevine (`quranic:root_001050:B001/m01`); jujube-like fruit (`quranic:root_001050:B003/m01`); small palm saplings (`quranic:root_000831:B008/m01`); small palm saplings (`quranic:root_000832:B006/m01`); leafy crop and aromatic plant (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); tree breaking again into leaf (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); farmer covering seed with soil (`quranic:root_001307:B008/m01`); willow tree (`quranic:root_000871:B006/m01`); chosen plant essence that can be bitter (`quranic:root_000889:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ع ن ب` (أَعْنَٰبًۭا); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ), `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا), `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:39 `ش ي ء` (شَآءَ); 78:40 `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ).
- Synthesis: Fruitfulness includes the whole vegetal cycle: covered seed, sapling, vine, fruit, leaf renewal, and plant essence. Bitter sap preserves a precise contrast between productive growth and an inedible extraction.

#### Subchannel C. Field Tool, Craftsman, and Vineyard Guard
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Workers fit a plough, shape wood with an adze, exercise manual skill, and post a guard who surveys the cultivated boundary.
- Active motifs: paired ploughing rods (`quranic:root_000741:B015/m01`); adze for shaping wood (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m01`); skilled manual craft (`quranic:root_001693:B015/m01`); vineyard guard or overseer (`quranic:root_001520:B009/m01`); intense fixed gaze (`quranic:root_000300:B004/m01`); stewardship and maintenance (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); repair and gradual cultivation (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ح د ق` (حَدَآئِقَ); 78:35 `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ), `ن ظ ر` (يَنظُرُ).
- Synthesis: Cultivation is completed by roles and instruments: craft shapes the tool, the worker maintains the field, and the guard visually enforces its perimeter. The garden is thus made and protected, not merely possessed.

#### Subchannel D. Rain, Cistern, Water Skin, and Night Approach
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rain descends, water is retained in a basin, carried in a skin, and approached at night by those whose travel and settlement depend on it.
- Active motifs: descending rain (`quranic:root_000889:B001/m01`); water-holding basin or cistern (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); abundant collected water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); water that secures a traveler’s affairs (`quranic:root_001444:B007/m01`); water skin (`quranic:root_001212:B009/m01`); nighttime approach to a water source (`quranic:root_001212:B008/m01`); assembly at water (`quranic:root_000871:B007/m01`); sweet potable water (`quranic:root_000994:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا), `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا), `ع ذ ب` (عَذَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: Water is an organized resource with source, storage, container, route, and gathering point. Its ability to “master the affair” of travelers shows why irrigation and settlement depend on control of flow.

### 21. P3 (78:31-40): Maturity, Peer Companionship, and Household Formation
- Semantic invariant: Bodily maturation is socially organized through peer equality, betrothal, marriage, dwelling, and the incorporation or separation of kin.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:33 names mature companions of equal age.
- Surprising reach: The scene reaches breast development, chest ornament, hymenal closure, marriage contract, stepchildren, square dwelling, intimacy, and irrevocable separation.

#### Subchannel A. Bodily Maturation and Ornamented Chest
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The body reaches visible maturity through breast development and projecting form, with chest and necklace placement marking the mature figure.
- Active motifs: breast reaching mature prominence (`quranic:root_001304:B003/m01`); ankle or bone projection (`quranic:root_001304:B001/m01`); chest bones and necklace position (`quranic:root_000178:B005/m01`); elevated honor or mature standing (`quranic:root_001304:B009/m01`); female circumcision site (`quranic:root_001050:B005/m01`); sealed hymenal state (`quranic:root_001304:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ع ن ب` (أَعْنَٰبًۭا); 78:33 `ك ع ب` (كَوَاعِبَ), `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: The surface term for mature companions is anatomically elaborated through projection, chest, and bodily threshold. Ornament and honor turn maturation into a publicly legible status.

#### Subchannel B. Equal-Age Peer and Matched Companion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Individuals raised together are recognized as age-peers, visual counterparts, and members of a balanced formation.
- Active motifs: equal-age companion raised together (`quranic:root_000178:B004/m01`); counterpart matched to its peer (`quranic:root_001520:B006/m01`); intermediate state between two degrees (`quranic:root_000170:B011/m01`); alignment on one level row (`quranic:root_000871:B001/m01`); exact equality of weight (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); mutual orientation in which each sees the other (`quranic:root_001520:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33 `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا); 78:37 `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا); 78:38 `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا), `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:40 `ن ظ ر` (يَنظُرُ).
- Synthesis: Peerhood is equality made relational: age, weight, position, and reciprocal visibility all match. The row gives that equality a spatial form without collapsing distinct persons.

#### Subchannel C. Betrothal, Marriage, Dwelling, and Kin Incorporation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A marriage is proposed and contracted, the couple enters a built dwelling, intimacy joins them, and existing children are incorporated into the household.
- Active motifs: proposal and betrothal (`quranic:root_000421:B002/m01`); marriage contract (`quranic:root_001444:B004/m01`); square elevated house (`quranic:root_001304:B002/m01`); stepchild under a new guardian (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); intimate approach (`quranic:root_001212:B007/m01`); close kin relation (`quranic:root_000552:B002/m01`); binding connection between parties (`quranic:root_000170:B003/m01`); irrevocable divorce cutting return (`quranic:root_000170:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33 `ك ع ب` (كَوَاعِبَ); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37 `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا), `خ ط ب` (خِطَابًۭا), `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ), `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: Household formation has a sequence of proposal, contract, dwelling, intimacy, and kin incorporation. Irrevocable separation is its structurally exact inverse because it cuts the possibility of return.

### 22. P3 (78:31-40): Brimful Cup, Intake, and Provisioned Satisfaction
- Semantic invariant: Abundance is contained, presented, taken in through the body, and judged sufficient by the drinker or host.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:34 gives a brimful cup and 78:36 calls the reward a measured gift.
- Surprising reach: Fullness reaches pressure between timbers, violent emptying, liquid circulation in a pot, the esophagus, celebratory feeding, hand-to-hand transfer, and a vessel’s claim that it has enough.

#### Subchannel A. Vessel Filled to the Rim and Emptied with Force
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A cup is filled to its upper limit, its contents circulate, and the same vessel can be forcefully emptied.
- Active motifs: cup together with its drink (`quranic:root_001277:B001/m01`); cup filled to the brim (`quranic:root_000495:B001/m01`); pressure and tight interpenetration (`quranic:root_000495:B002/m01`); forceful emptying (`quranic:root_000495:B003/m01`); morsel circulating in a boiling pot (`quranic:root_000495:B005/m01`); filling a thing completely (`quranic:root_001304:B007/m01`); wine (`quranic:root_000609:B015/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33 `ك ع ب` (كَوَاعِبَ); 78:34 `ك ء س` (كَأْسًۭا), `د ه ق` (دِهَاقًۭا); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ).
- Synthesis: “Brimful” is a capacity operation: the container receives pressure until its upper limit is reached. Circulation and emptying then describe the possible movement of what the cup contains.

#### Subchannel B. Palatable Food, Throat Passage, and Festive Feeding
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Food or drink is taken, passes through the esophagus, is experienced as pleasant, and extends into feeding at a household celebration.
- Active motifs: palatable easy food (`quranic:root_001409:B003/m01`); palatable easy food (`quranic:root_001410:B003/m01`); esophagus carrying food and drink (`quranic:root_001409:B004/m01`); esophageal channel (`quranic:root_001410:B005/m01`); eating and celebratory feeding (`quranic:root_001410:B004/m01`); food and feeding (`quranic:root_001409:B005/m01`); taking by hand (`quranic:root_001028:B001/m01`); hand-to-hand delivery (`quranic:root_001693:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:34 `ك ء س` (كَأْسًۭا); 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً); 78:40 `م ر ء` (ٱلْمَرْءُ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Provision becomes bodily and social: it is handed over, swallowed through a named channel, and judged pleasant. Household feeding extends private intake into communal celebration.

#### Subchannel C. Sufficiency Spoken by Vessel and Receiver
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A filled container or recipient indicates that capacity has been met, and the provider’s measure is recognized as adequate.
- Active motifs: thing declaring “enough” by its state (`quranic:root_001272:B014/m01`); sufficiency and adequacy (`quranic:root_000318:B003/m01`); amount near a full measure (`quranic:root_001212:B016/m01`); measured handful or sum of wealth (`quranic:root_000495:B006/m01`); gift handed over (`quranic:root_001028:B002/m01`); thing received and held (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:34 `د ه ق` (دِهَاقًۭا); 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً), `ح س ب` (حِسَابًۭا); 78:38, 78:40 `ق و ل` (قَالَ/يَقُولُ); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: Fullness communicates without requiring a separate speaker: the vessel’s condition says “enough.” Gift, measure, near-full quantity, and receipt establish sufficiency as a relation between capacity and provision.

### 23. P3 (78:31-40): Measured Grant, Service, and Status Reversal
- Semantic invariant: A sovereignly sourced grant answers conduct, passes through acts of service and handover, and changes the recipient’s condition from need to sufficiency.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:36 combines recompense, gift, and measure.
- Surprising reach: The grant is reframed through debt settlement, substitution, domestic service, compliant instruments, begging, soil-bound poverty, abundant wealth, and a hand’s power to give.

#### Subchannel A. Recompense, Account, and Debt Settlement
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Conduct is evaluated, an answering value is computed, and the resulting liability or credit is fully settled.
- Active motifs: reward or punishment answering an act (`quranic:root_000244:B001/m01`); substitute discharging another’s obligation (`quranic:root_000244:B002/m01`); debt collection and settlement (`quranic:root_000244:B003/m01`); fiscal discharge (`quranic:root_000244:B004/m01`); prevailing in reciprocal settlement (`quranic:root_000244:B005/m01`); arithmetic account (`quranic:root_000318:B001/m01`); practical oversight and accounting (`quranic:root_000318:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ), `ح س ب` (حِسَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: Recompense is a settlement mechanism with evaluation, substitution, debt, and closure. The account specifies the amount; recompense transfers it to the side on which it is due.

#### Subchannel B. Giving, Serving, and Compliant Handover
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A giver presents an object, a servant brings what is needed, and a compliant recipient or tool yields to the transfer.
- Active motifs: taking and raising by hand (`quranic:root_001028:B001/m01`); giving and presenting (`quranic:root_001028:B002/m01`); service and bringing requested things (`quranic:root_001028:B003/m01`); pliancy and compliant bending (`quranic:root_001028:B006/m01`); beneficent hand (`quranic:root_001693:B003/m01`); immediate hand-to-hand exchange (`quranic:root_001693:B007/m01`); care and gradual provision (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً), `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Gift is a coordinated transfer involving giver, servant, object, and receiver. Pliancy matters because successful handover requires the receiving side or instrument to accommodate what is offered.

#### Subchannel C. Ownership, Entitlement, and Restored Right
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A thing belongs to an owner, a right becomes due, and what has been withheld is restored to its proper holder.
- Active motifs: possession and power of disposal (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); sovereign dominion (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); private right held against another (`quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`); fixed entitlement (`quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`); right returned to its owner (`quranic:root_000609:B006/m01`); owned or controlled hand (`quranic:root_001693:B004/m01`); grant standing in another’s place (`quranic:root_000244:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءً); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Giving is distinguished from returning what is already due. Ownership, right, disposal, and restoration define whether a transfer is gift, settlement, or restitution.

#### Subchannel D. Soil-Bound Need Reversed into Wealth and Spacious Living
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A needy person is pressed to the ground, asks for provision, receives a measured amount, and rises into wealth or spacious living.
- Active motifs: poverty through clinging to dust (`quranic:root_000178:B002/m01`); wealth through abundance of earth or property (`quranic:root_000178:B003/m01`); need or want (`quranic:root_000532:B016/m01`); blessing and benefaction (`quranic:root_000532:B016/m02`); asking people for a gift (`quranic:root_001028:B005/m01`); handful or sum of money (`quranic:root_000495:B006/m01`); spacious livelihood (`quranic:root_001693:B014/m01`); inherited honor and resources (`quranic:root_000318:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33, 78:40 `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا/تُرَٰبًۢا); 78:34 `د ه ق` (دِهَاقًۭا); 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً), `ح س ب` (حِسَابًۭا), `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Need and wealth are reversible states built from the same soil image. Asking, measured money, blessing, and spacious living provide the transfer sequence by which a ground-bound condition can be changed.

### 24. P3 (78:31-40): Hearing Protected from Noise, Abuse, and False Report
- Semantic invariant: Auditory well-being depends on an open receptive organ, attentive understanding, and the exclusion of disruptive or deceptive sound.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:35 denies both idle speech and falsehood to the inhabitants.
- Surprising reach: The quiet scene includes the ear as vessel-handle, listening as obedience, rumor and public shame, mixed noise, animal cries, pleasurable song, and the formula for a bad report that is heard but not allowed to arrive.

#### Subchannel A. Ear, Attention, Understanding, and Obedient Reception
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Sound reaches the ear, is actively attended to, becomes understood, and can issue in acceptance or compliance.
- Active motifs: hearing sound through the ear (`quranic:root_000741:B001/m01`); ear and auditory opening (`quranic:root_000741:B002/m01`); understanding and obeying what is heard (`quranic:root_000741:B003/m01`); causing another to hear (`quranic:root_000741:B004/m01`); ear and ear-shaped handle (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); attentive acceptance (`quranic:root_000022:B002/m01`); listening expressed through the will-root (`quranic:root_000831:B006/m01`); listening (`quranic:root_000832:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:35 `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ); 78:38 `ء ذ ن` (أَذِنَ); 78:39 `ش ي ء` (شَآءَ).
- Synthesis: Hearing is a staged reception process: aperture, sound, attention, understanding, and acceptance. The ear-as-handle makes reception materially graspable, while obedience marks successful semantic uptake.

#### Subchannel B. Idle Speech, Mixed Noise, and Public Abuse
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech loses informational value, blends into noise, and becomes insult, rumor, or public harm.
- Active motifs: discarded utterance that does not count (`quranic:root_001361:B001/m01`); obscene or abusive speech (`quranic:root_001361:B002/m01`); mixed loud noise and animal cries (`quranic:root_001361:B003/m01`); compulsive repetition and group idiom (`quranic:root_001361:B004/m01`); deviation from correctness (`quranic:root_001361:B005/m01`); public reputation or ostentatious hearing (`quranic:root_000741:B005/m01`); making another hear insult (`quranic:root_000741:B006/m01`); circulating public talk (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:35 `ل غ و` (لَغْوًۭا), `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ); 78:38, 78:40 `ق و ل` (قَالَ/يَقُولُ).
- Synthesis: Laghw is defined by failed communicative function: it does not count, does not stay on the right course, and overwhelms intelligibility with repetition or volume. Abuse and rumor add social damage to acoustic disorder.

#### Subchannel C. Pleasant Sound, Silence, and Contained Bad News
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Enjoyable sound is distinguished from disruptive noise, while a harmful report is contained before it reaches its target and ordered bodies remain silent.
- Active motifs: pleasurable song or beautiful voice (`quranic:root_000741:B007/m01`); formula “heard but not delivered” for bad news (`quranic:root_000741:B011/m01`); standing upright without motion (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); orderly row (`quranic:root_000871:B001/m01`); falsehood as rejected report (`quranic:root_001290:B001/m01`); attributing a report to lying (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:35 `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ), `ك ذ ب` (كِذَّٰبًۭا); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ), `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا).
- Synthesis: Quiet is not the absence of all sound: pleasurable sound remains intelligible and bounded. Harmful news, false report, and disorderly noise are instead stopped, while the row gives silence a disciplined bodily form.

### 25. P3 (78:31-40): Utterance Moving from Interior Thought to Licensed Correct Speech
- Semantic invariant: Potential speech begins as inward formulation, acquires linguistic form, passes an authority threshold, and is admitted only when it reaches a correct target.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:37 denies possession of address, and 78:38 permits speech only by authorization and with correctness.
- Surprising reach: Speech is a manufactured line involving tongue, word, clause, inward rehearsal, negotiation, permission, ruler, target, woven integrity, and deviation.

#### Subchannel A. Tongue, Word, Address, and Clarifying Expression
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An inner meaning is articulated through tongue and word into an address that makes its object clear to another.
- Active motifs: reciprocal address and formal speech (`quranic:root_000421:B001/m01`); spoken utterance (`quranic:root_001272:B001/m01`); tongue as instrument of saying (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); intelligible exchange between speakers (`quranic:root_001316:B001/m01`); meaningful word or extended expression (`quranic:root_001316:B002/m01`); clarification by speech, writing, or sign (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`); manifest explanation (`quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا), `خ ط ب` (خِطَابًۭا); 78:38 `ك ل م` (يَتَكَلَّمُونَ), `ق و ل` (قَالَ).
- Synthesis: Speech is an embodied transmission chain: tongue externalizes a meaningful unit, address relates speakers, and explanation discloses the intended object. Word and extended discourse remain distinct scales within the same communicative operation.

#### Subchannel B. Interior Saying, Attribution, and Circulating Falsehood
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A saying is rehearsed inwardly, appropriated or attributed, and may emerge as rumor, fabrication, or an imposed statement never made by its alleged speaker.
- Active motifs: inward unspoken saying (`quranic:root_001272:B012/m01`); drawing a saying into oneself (`quranic:root_001272:B006/m01`); fabricating or attributing words not spoken (`quranic:root_001272:B005/m01`); circulating talk among people (`quranic:root_001272:B007/m01`); saying used as conjecture (`quranic:root_001272:B011/m01`); falsehood assigned to a speaker (`quranic:root_001290:B002/m01`); lying self (`quranic:root_001290:B008/m01`); lamenting or exclaiming call (`quranic:root_000831:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:35 `ك ذ ب` (كِذَّٰبًۭا); 78:38, 78:40 `ق و ل` (قَالَ/يَقُولُ); 78:39 `ش ي ء` (شَآءَ).
- Synthesis: The path from thought to public statement can be corrupted at several points: inward rehearsal becomes appropriation, attribution, rumor, or conjecture. The channel therefore distinguishes silence from the active manufacture of false speech.

#### Subchannel C. Permission, Gatekeeping, and Sovereign Access
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A petitioner approaches an authority, requests entry to speech, and may speak only after the controlling power authorizes access.
- Active motifs: permission and authorization (`quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`); public notice or formal announcement (`quranic:root_000022:B003/m01`); sovereign dominion (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); ownership and disposal (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); ruler whose word has effect (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); courtly nearness to a king (`quranic:root_001212:B004/m01`); merciful regard (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`); controlled address (`quranic:root_000421:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `خ ط ب` (خِطَابًۭا), `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ), `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ); 78:38 `ء ذ ن` (أَذِنَ), `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنُ), `م ل ك` (ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ), `ق و ل` (قَالَ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: Permission is a threshold relation between would-be speaker and sovereign. Ownership of the speech-event remains with the authority, while nearness and mercy shape who may cross that threshold.

#### Subchannel D. Correct Utterance as Targeted, Upright, and Well-Woven
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Speech is accepted when it aligns with reality, travels toward its proper target, and holds together without deviation.
- Active motifs: correct speech or action opposite error (`quranic:root_000889:B002/m01`); aiming and striking the intended target (`quranic:root_000889:B003/m01`); deviation or turning aside (`quranic:root_000889:B009/m01`); truth matching what is fixed (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); proving and manifesting truth (`quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`); tightly woven or well-formed discourse (`quranic:root_000347:B010/m01`); upright and balanced expression (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); clarifying meaning (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ), `ق و ل` (قَالَ), `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ).
- Synthesis: Correctness is more than sincerity: the utterance must correspond to what is fixed, hold its structure, and reach the right object. Targeting, uprightness, and weave make accuracy a material alignment.

### 26. P3 (78:31-40): Sovereign Domain, Ordered Court, and Restricted Audience
- Semantic invariant: A ruler sustains a bounded domain, arranges participants into ranks, and controls both proximity and address within the court.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:36-38 names the Lord of heavens and earth, denies autonomous address, and depicts the Spirit and angels standing in a row.
- Surprising reach: Dominion is assembled from wall-strength, ownership, road center, water provision, stewarding, body-height, military row, courtier access, and effective royal speech.

#### Subchannel A. Ownership, Dominion, and Sustaining Administration
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The sovereign owns and strengthens the domain, maintains its affairs, delegates guidance, and supplies what permits subjects to remain.
- Active motifs: lordship, ownership, and mastery (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`); repair, nurture, and completion (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m02`); material strength holding a wall or self together (`quranic:root_001444:B001/m01`); ownership and disposal (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); kingship and public rule (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); indispensable mainstay (`quranic:root_001444:B005/m01`); leading animal or guide (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); stewardship and governance (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); sovereign hand (`quranic:root_001693:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Sovereignty combines title with operation: ownership, structural strength, maintenance, guidance, and provision keep the domain coherent. The mainstay image prevents rule from being reduced to command alone.

#### Subchannel B. Heavens, Earth, and the Interval Between
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: An upper covering and lower ground define a vertical domain, while the interval between them is measured, traversed, and held within the same rule.
- Active motifs: elevation and height (`quranic:root_000745:B001/m01`); visible raised form (`quranic:root_000745:B002/m01`); overhead sky and covering (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); lower earth opposite sky (`quranic:root_000025:B001/m01`); middle interval between two sides (`quranic:root_000170:B002/m01`); broad distance between limits (`quranic:root_000170:B006/m01`); tract of land extending to sight (`quranic:root_000170:B007/m01`); downward direction (`quranic:root_000889:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `س م و` (ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ), `ء ر ض` (ٱلْأَرْضِ), `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا); 78:38 `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: The domain is geometrically complete: above, below, and the measurable interval separating them. Distance and visible tract make “between” a positive governed space rather than an empty remainder.

#### Subchannel C. Standing Rows, Rank, and Resurrection Assembly
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Participants rise, take assigned places in a level row, and form a stable assembly oriented toward the sovereign.
- Active motifs: bodily standing upright (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`); gathered people or retinue (`quranic:root_001273:B001/m01`); place of standing or residence (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`); stature and bodily height (`quranic:root_001273:B011/m01`); resurrection and assembly for the Lord (`quranic:root_001273:B013/m01`); alignment on a straight row (`quranic:root_000871:B001/m01`); level bare parade ground (`quranic:root_000871:B005/m01`); life-bearing spirit (`quranic:root_000609:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ), `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ), `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا).
- Synthesis: Courtly order is produced by posture, place, stature, and alignment. Resurrection supplies the maximal assembly in which standing is both bodily reanimation and assumption of a fixed rank.

#### Subchannel D. Courtier Nearness and Controlled Address
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Courtiers approach the ruler, but proximity does not itself grant authority to address; effective speech remains licensed and rank-sensitive.
- Active motifs: favored courtier near the king (`quranic:root_001212:B004/m01`); ruler whose saying carries force (`quranic:root_001272:B004/m01`); formal address (`quranic:root_000421:B001/m01`); grave matter that elicits address (`quranic:root_000421:B003/m01`); permission to enter or speak (`quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`); competitive ranking (`quranic:root_000745:B007/m01`); elevation of social standing (`quranic:root_000745:B001/m02`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `خ ط ب` (خِطَابًۭا), `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ); 78:38 `ء ذ ن` (أَذِنَ), `ق و ل` (قَالَ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: Nearness, rank, and speech-right are distinct court relations. A courtier may be physically and socially close, yet only authorization converts presence into effective address.

### 27. P3 (78:31-40): Mercy Expressed as Kinship, Womb, and Nurture
- Semantic invariant: Merciful care protects vulnerable life through relational closeness, gestational enclosure, birth, and continued upbringing.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:37-38 names the Merciful, while the pericope’s reward and gift supply the action of beneficent care.
- Surprising reach: Mercy becomes womb-space, blood relation, foster household, nearing birth, postpartum pain and discharge, fresh milk, and gradual cultivation.

#### Subchannel A. Compassion, Benefaction, and Gradual Care
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A powerful caregiver bends toward a vulnerable recipient, gives what is needed, and brings the recipient toward completion over time.
- Active motifs: tenderness, compassion, and beneficence (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`); gradual nurture and repair (`quranic:root_000532:B002/m02`); giving and presenting (`quranic:root_001028:B002/m01`); service that brings another’s needs (`quranic:root_001028:B003/m01`); beneficent hand (`quranic:root_001693:B003/m01`); blessing and favor (`quranic:root_000532:B016/m02`); lively generosity toward good (`quranic:root_000609:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً); 78:37-38 `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ/ٱلرَّحْمَٰنُ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Mercy is an active care process rather than an isolated feeling. Compassion initiates giving, service supplies particulars, and nurture gradually repairs or completes the recipient.

#### Subchannel B. Womb, Blood Relation, and Foster Household
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Life is enclosed in the womb, born into a blood relation, and incorporated into a household that may include fostered or stepchildren.
- Active motifs: womb as the enclosed site of growth (`quranic:root_000552:B003/m01`); close kinship and blood relation (`quranic:root_000552:B002/m01`); kin nearness (`quranic:root_001212:B003/m01`); stepchild and caregiver (`quranic:root_000532:B005/m01`); equal-age companion raised alongside another (`quranic:root_000178:B004/m01`); relational bond joining parties (`quranic:root_000170:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33, 78:40 `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا/تُرَٰبًۢا); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37 `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا), `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: Mercy’s relational infrastructure is womb, kinship, and household. Biological nearness and fostered care are not identical, but both create the durable bond within which vulnerable life is maintained.

#### Subchannel C. Near Birth, Postpartum Pain, and Fresh Milk
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Gestation reaches its due point, birth occurs, the mother passes through pain and discharge, and new milk sustains the young.
- Active motifs: pregnancy nearing delivery (`quranic:root_001212:B012/m01`); womb pain after birth (`quranic:root_000552:B004/m01`); postpartum discharge (`quranic:root_000994:B009/m01`); womb (`quranic:root_000994:B009/m02`); recently delivered milk-animal (`quranic:root_000532:B009/m01`); female animal collecting multiple milk streams (`quranic:root_000871:B002/m01`); animal reaching mature breeding condition (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); completed bodily strength (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ/ٱلرَّحْمَٰنُ); 78:38 `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا), `ع ذ ب` (عَذَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: Care is tested at the transition from enclosure to exposed life. Near birth, maternal pain, bodily discharge, and fresh milk trace the costly process by which gestation becomes sustained offspring.

### 28. P3 (78:31-40): Life-Breath Expanding into Wind, Scent, and Relief
- Semantic invariant: An animating current moves through body and world as life, air, force, fragrance, vegetative renewal, and restored ease.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:38 names the Spirit standing before the Merciful.
- Surprising reach: The root’s reach assembles breath, death, wind over water, odor transfer, evening return, alternating labor, open stance, generosity, wine, and trees breaking into leaf.

#### Subchannel A. Animating Spirit and Its Withdrawal
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Breath animates the body; its presence supports life and its withdrawal ends bodily activity.
- Active motifs: soul or spirit animating the body (`quranic:root_000609:B001/m01`); death expressed as being relieved (`quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`); salvation and attainment of good (`quranic:root_001186:B001/m01`); death or passing away (`quranic:root_001186:B002/m01`); occurrence of a bodily state (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`); human being as embodied person (`quranic:root_001409:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:31 `ف و ز` (مَفَازًا); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:40 `ك و ن` (كُنتُ), `م ر ء` (ٱلْمَرْءُ).
- Synthesis: Life and death are separated by the presence or withdrawal of an animating current. “Relief” as death presses on salvation by showing that release can mean either attained good or departure from embodied life.

#### Subchannel B. Wind, Cloud, Rain, and Force
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Air moves across the domain, gathers cloud, descends as rain, and supplies either generative circulation or overpowering force.
- Active motifs: wind and moving air (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); wind as strength and victory (`quranic:root_000609:B011/m01`); layered rain cloud (`quranic:root_000532:B008/m01`); sky and rain-bearing cover (`quranic:root_000745:B004/m01`); rainfall descending from above (`quranic:root_000889:B001/m01`); open exposure to the sky (`quranic:root_000994:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37 `س م و` (ٱلسَّمَٰوَٰتِ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ), `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:40 `ع ذ ب` (عَذَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: Air is the medium linking sky, cloud, rain, and force. The same current can irrigate exposed ground or become the power by which one side prevails.

#### Subchannel C. Odor Transfer, Camphor, and Vegetative Emanation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Plants and substances release odors that pass into surrounding material, while renewed leaf and aromatic growth announce life.
- Active motifs: scent, smell, and odor acquired from another thing (`quranic:root_000609:B004/m01`); aromatic crop and leaves (`quranic:root_000609:B012/m01`); tree breaking into fresh foliage (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`); camphor perfume (`quranic:root_001307:B011/m01`); grapevine and fruit (`quranic:root_001050:B001/m01`); sweet palatable substance (`quranic:root_000994:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ع ن ب` (أَعْنَٰبًۭا); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:40 `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ), `ع ذ ب` (عَذَابًۭا).
- Synthesis: Life becomes perceptible by emanation: leaf, fruit, camphor, and scent alter the air and nearby material. Odor transfer is the olfactory counterpart to visible ripening.

#### Subchannel D. Evening Return, Alternating Labor, and Restored Ease
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Labor alternates between participants, living beings return at evening, and the tired body recovers through rest and expanded stance.
- Active motifs: evening return of people or livestock (`quranic:root_000609:B005/m01`); rest and recovery after fatigue (`quranic:root_000609:B007/m01`); alternation between workers or tasks (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); bodily breadth and open stance (`quranic:root_000609:B009/m01`); generous energetic responsiveness (`quranic:root_000609:B010/m01`); stoppage and exhaustion (`quranic:root_001273:B016/m01`); residence and return (`quranic:root_000065:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ), `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ء و ب` (مَـَٔابًا).
- Synthesis: Relief is cyclical rather than inert: tasks alternate, bodies return, stance opens, and capacity is restored. Exhausted stoppage is the failure condition against which genuine rest is measured.

### 29. P3 (78:31-40): Truth as Reality, Entitlement, and Fitted Trajectory
- Semantic invariant: What is true remains fixed, becomes demonstrable, establishes enforceable rights, and fits its proper path or joint without deviation.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:38 requires correct speech and 78:39 names the day as the truth.
- Surprising reach: Truth behaves like a proven claim, protected property, penetrating thrust, tightly woven cloth, fitted joint, equal weight, straight road, and accurately aimed projectile.

#### Subchannel A. Fixed Reality, Proof, and Manifest Clarity
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A claim corresponds to stable reality, is demonstrated, and becomes plainly visible in speech or sign.
- Active motifs: truth fixed against falsehood (`quranic:root_000347:B001/m01`); proving and manifesting truth (`quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`); tightly verified speech (`quranic:root_000347:B010/m01`); appearing and becoming clear (`quranic:root_000170:B004/m01`); explanation that discloses meaning (`quranic:root_000170:B005/m01`); correct statement opposite error (`quranic:root_000889:B002/m01`); knowable object of a report (`quranic:root_000831:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `ب ي ن` (بَيْنَهُمَا); 78:38 `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ), `ش ي ء` (شَآءَ).
- Synthesis: Truth is a relation between statement, evidence, and what remains fixed. Manifestation and clarification make that relation publicly inspectable rather than privately asserted.

#### Subchannel B. Obligation, Private Right, and Required Protection
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fixed reality generates a due obligation, belongs to a claimant, and imposes a duty of defense or restitution.
- Active motifs: binding obligation and deserved claim (`quranic:root_000347:B002/m01`); private right owned by a claimant (`quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`); protected charge or inviolable responsibility (`quranic:root_000347:B007/m01`); returning a right to its owner (`quranic:root_000609:B006/m01`); possession and disposal (`quranic:root_001444:B002/m01`); collecting what is owed (`quranic:root_000244:B003/m01`); warning obligation voluntarily assumed (`quranic:root_001488:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ن ذ ر` (أَنذَرْنَٰكُمْ).
- Synthesis: Truth creates normative consequences: something becomes due, belongs to someone, and must be protected or returned. The channel thus moves from correspondence with reality to enforceable entitlement.

#### Subchannel C. Penetrating Line, Fitted Joint, and Upright Measure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A straight action reaches its target, passes without deflection, and settles into a matching joint or equal measure.
- Active motifs: thrust penetrating straight into the interior (`quranic:root_000347:B009/m01`); fitted meeting of bone and socket (`quranic:root_000347:B011/m01`); aimed action striking the target (`quranic:root_000889:B003/m01`); uprightness and rectitude (`quranic:root_001273:B008/m01`); equal balanced weight (`quranic:root_001273:B015/m01`); node between two shafts (`quranic:root_001304:B005/m01`); converging step of a horse (`quranic:root_000347:B014/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33 `ك ع ب` (كَوَاعِبَ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ), `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ).
- Synthesis: Correctness is rendered as mechanical fit and undeviating trajectory. The thrust, target, joint, step, and equal weight all succeed because line and receptacle correspond.

#### Subchannel D. Rights Dispute, Negotiation, and Adjudicative Contest
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Rival claimants assert possession of the truth, negotiate or contest the matter, and submit it to comparative examination.
- Active motifs: reciprocal claim that each side has the right (`quranic:root_000347:B004/m01`); negotiation over an affair (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); imposing judgment on another (`quranic:root_001272:B010/m01`); examining a matter together (`quranic:root_001520:B010/m01`); resistance and contest (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); prevailing in recompense (`quranic:root_000244:B005/m01`); prevailing in reciprocal handling (`quranic:root_001028:B007/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ), `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً); 78:38, 78:40 `ق و ل` (قَالَ/يَقُولُ), `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ن ظ ر` (يَنظُرُ).
- Synthesis: A right can be true yet socially contested. Claim, negotiation, examination, resistance, and judgment define the process by which rival assertions are tested rather than merely exchanged.

### 30. P3 (78:31-40): Deliberate Choice Becoming an Adopted Route of Return
- Semantic invariant: A subject perceives available possibilities, forms a will, takes up a course, and approaches a destination to which movement ultimately returns.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:39 joins willing, taking, the Lord, and a return-place, while 78:40 declares the destination temporally near.
- Surprising reach: Choice becomes attraction, deliberation, grip, acquisition, advance, homecoming gait, sunset return, kin nearness, court access, sacrifice, and near-full measure.

#### Subchannel A. Will, Attraction, and Deliberative Attention
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A possible object draws the subject, is examined, and becomes the focus of a formed intention.
- Active motifs: will directed toward bringing about a thing (`quranic:root_000831:B002/m01`); volition (`quranic:root_000832:B001/m01`); attraction and delight toward an object (`quranic:root_000831:B005/m01`); reflective visual or intellectual inspection (`quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`); inward care directed toward a thing (`quranic:root_001272:B015/m01`); resolve to undertake an affair (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:38, 78:40 `ق و ل` (قَالَ/يَقُولُ), `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ش ي ء` (شَآءَ); 78:40 `ن ظ ر` (يَنظُرُ).
- Synthesis: Choice begins before outward movement: attention, attraction, care, and resolve organize a possibility into intention. The doubled lexical witness to will reinforces volition without collapsing it into desire alone.

#### Subchannel B. Taking, Acquiring, and Entering a Course
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: Intention becomes action when the subject grasps, adopts, acquires, and deliberately advances along a chosen course.
- Active motifs: taking and holding an object (`quranic:root_000018:B001/m01`); adopting or acquiring for oneself (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`); intentional aim toward a thing (`quranic:root_001207:B010/m01`); rising to undertake action (`quranic:root_001273:B003/m01`); owned capacity and disposal (`quranic:root_001693:B004/m01`); moving forward and preceding (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`); bodily grip or handle (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: “Take” is the hinge between volition and route. Grasp, acquisition, aim, and advance convert an inward decision into a course that the subject now owns and enacts.

#### Subchannel C. Homecoming, Repeated Gait, and Destination
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The traveler’s limbs repeat a returning motion, the traveler arrives from a journey, and movement resolves in a place of return.
- Active motifs: return to one’s abode (`quranic:root_000065:B001/m01`); returning motion of limbs (`quranic:root_000065:B003/m01`); approach from every direction (`quranic:root_000065:B004/m01`); night return (`quranic:root_000065:B006/m01`); sun returning to its setting (`quranic:root_000065:B007/m01`); arrival home from travel (`quranic:root_001207:B005/m01`); residence and persistence (`quranic:root_000532:B007/m01`); standing-place or abode (`quranic:root_001273:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ء و ب` (مَـَٔابًا); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ).
- Synthesis: Return is not only endpoint but repeated bodily motion, temporal rhythm, and settled residence. Sunset and night-return provide cyclical analogues for the traveler’s final homecoming.

#### Subchannel D. Nearness in Time, Kinship, Court, and Devotion
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A destination approaches temporally and spatially, while relational or ritual acts reduce social distance to kin, sovereign, or Lord.
- Active motifs: nearness opposite distance (`quranic:root_001212:B001/m01`); approaching time or account (`quranic:root_001212:B002/m01`); kinship nearness (`quranic:root_001212:B003/m01`); favored access to a king (`quranic:root_001212:B004/m01`); devotional offering seeking nearness (`quranic:root_001212:B005/m01`); approximate fullness or amount (`quranic:root_001212:B016/m01`); severe event of the day (`quranic:root_001700:B003/m01`); grave approaching affair (`quranic:root_000421:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37 `خ ط ب` (خِطَابًۭا); 78:39 `ي و م` (ٱلْيَوْمُ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا), `ي و م` (يَوْمَ).
- Synthesis: Nearness is measured in several coordinate systems: distance, time, kin relation, court access, devotion, and quantity. Each reduces an interval, clarifying how an approaching destination can be both temporal and relational.

### 31. P3 (78:31-40): The Witnessing Body Sees, Acts, and Encounters Its Own Deed
- Semantic invariant: A person receives evidence through embodied perception, executes action through limbs and tools, and finally confronts what those actions have sent forward.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:40 depicts the person seeing what the hands have advanced and then speaking.
- Surprising reach: Witnessing recruits pupil, ear, brain, tongue, esophagus, chest, palm, foot, tool-handle, craft skill, bodily precedence, and the work attributed emphatically to two hands.

#### Subchannel A. Directed Gaze, Pupil, and Visible Appearance
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The observer directs sight toward an object, fixes attention, and receives its shape through the eye’s viewing point.
- Active motifs: visual and reflective inspection (`quranic:root_001520:B001/m01`); appearance presented to an observer (`quranic:root_001520:B004/m01`); pupil and viewing point in the eye (`quranic:root_001520:B008/m01`); pupil or dark center of the eye (`quranic:root_000300:B002/m01`); intense fixed gaze (`quranic:root_000300:B004/m01`); speculative seeing that mistakes appearance (`quranic:root_000741:B013/m01`); open land where no one hears or sees (`quranic:root_000741:B014/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ح د ق` (حَدَآئِقَ); 78:35 `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ); 78:40 `ن ظ ر` (يَنظُرُ).
- Synthesis: Seeing is an oriented bodily act with observer, pupil, target, and presented appearance. The speculative viewer and empty land preserve two failure modes: misrecognition and absence of any witness.

#### Subchannel B. Ear, Brain, Tongue, Throat, and Chest
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Sensation enters through ear and eye, is processed inwardly, and exits or passes through tongue and throat within a ribbed torso.
- Active motifs: ear as sensory aperture (`quranic:root_000022:B001/m01`); brain called the mother of hearing (`quranic:root_000741:B016/m01`); tongue as instrument of saying (`quranic:root_001272:B002/m01`); esophagus carrying intake (`quranic:root_001409:B004/m01`); chest and necklace bones (`quranic:root_000178:B005/m01`); palm of the hand (`quranic:root_000609:B014/m01`); eye’s dark viewing point (`quranic:root_001520:B008/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33, 78:40 `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا/تُرَٰبًۢا); 78:35 `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ); 78:38 `ء ذ ن` (أَذِنَ), `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ), `ق و ل` (قَالَ); 78:40 `م ر ء` (ٱلْمَرْءُ), `ن ظ ر` (يَنظُرُ).
- Synthesis: The person is rendered as a connected sensorimotor interior rather than an abstract moral subject. Ear and eye admit, brain processes, tongue externalizes, throat receives, and chest houses the system.

#### Subchannel C. Hand, Foot, Handle, and Skilled Capacity
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Limbs supply force and placement, while fitted handles and learned skill extend the body into effective tools.
- Active motifs: physical hand (`quranic:root_001693:B001/m01`); strength expressed as hand (`quranic:root_001693:B002/m01`); handle or projecting end of a tool (`quranic:root_001693:B013/m01`); manual skill (`quranic:root_001693:B015/m01`); grasping handle (`quranic:root_000018:B012/m01`); foot used for stepping (`quranic:root_001207:B001/m01`); adze for working wood (`quranic:root_001207:B008/m01`); taking by hand (`quranic:root_001028:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Agency is distributed across limb, grip, handle, and skill. Hand and foot position the body, while the tool extends force into shaped work.

#### Subchannel D. Forwarded Act, Earned Result, and Embodied Accountability
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: The subject advances an act ahead of the body, acquires its result, and later finds that result attributed back to the acting hands.
- Active motifs: what lies ahead or immediately before (`quranic:root_001693:B008/m01`); work and offense emphatically assigned to both hands (`quranic:root_001693:B009/m01`); prior deed or standing (`quranic:root_001207:B002/m01`); advancing and preceding (`quranic:root_001207:B004/m01`); front or vanguard (`quranic:root_001207:B007/m01`); deliberate aim toward an act (`quranic:root_001207:B010/m01`); acquisition through taking (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`); occurrence becoming present (`quranic:root_001332:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ), `ك و ن` (كُنتُ).
- Synthesis: A deed acquires spatial precedence: it is sent ahead, remains there, and becomes present to its maker later. Attribution to the hands closes the causal loop between embodied agency and encountered outcome.

### 32. P3 (78:31-40): Covering Truth, Covering Seed, and Returning to Soil
- Semantic invariant: Covering can hide truth destructively, protect growth constructively, erase fault ritually, or receive the body at death.
- Surface relation: direct; 78:40 names the unbeliever and ends with the wish to be soil.
- Surprising reach: One covering operation spans armor, night and sea, denial, ingratitude, seed burial, fruit husk, expiation, camphor, remote village, mountain recess, grave, and shrouded return.

#### Subchannel A. Concealment, Denial, and Ingratitude
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A covering blocks what should remain visible, becoming denial of truth and concealment of received benefit.
- Active motifs: covering an object or weapon (`quranic:root_001307:B001/m01`); engulfing cover of night, sea, or great river (`quranic:root_001307:B002/m01`); concealing and rejecting truth (`quranic:root_001307:B003/m01`); covering a benefit through ingratitude (`quranic:root_001307:B004/m01`); repudiation and disavowal (`quranic:root_001307:B005/m01`); assigning unbelief to another (`quranic:root_001307:B006/m01`); forcing another toward disobedience (`quranic:root_001307:B007/m01`); truth made manifest against concealment (`quranic:root_000347:B005/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ).
- Synthesis: Unbelief is an active covering of available truth and benefit. Repudiation, attribution, and coercion show that the cover can be maintained socially as well as inwardly.

#### Subchannel B. Seed Burial, Fruit Sheath, and Productive Concealment
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A farmer covers seed with earth, the plant germinates unseen, and a sheath protects developing fruit until exposure is useful.
- Active motifs: farmer covering seed with soil (`quranic:root_001307:B008/m01`); fruit sheath or protective bract (`quranic:root_001307:B010/m01`); earth and dust as covering material (`quranic:root_000178:B001/m01`); palm sapling (`quranic:root_000831:B008/m01`); small palm saplings (`quranic:root_000832:B006/m01`); enclosed garden (`quranic:root_000300:B003/m01`); plant breaking into leaf (`quranic:root_000609:B017/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ح د ق` (حَدَآئِقَ); 78:33, 78:40 `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا/تُرَٰبًۢا); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:39 `ش ي ء` (شَآءَ); 78:40 `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ).
- Synthesis: This covering preserves rather than suppresses life. Soil and sheath control the timing of exposure so that hidden growth can mature before it emerges.

#### Subchannel C. Expiatory Cover and Fragrant Purification
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A fault is covered until its effect is removed, while offering and fragrant substance mark a restored relation.
- Active motifs: expiation covering a fault (`quranic:root_001307:B009/m01`); camphor perfume (`quranic:root_001307:B011/m01`); devotional offering seeking nearness (`quranic:root_001212:B005/m01`); compassion and beneficence (`quranic:root_000552:B001/m01`); substitution discharging another’s obligation (`quranic:root_000244:B002/m01`); fragrant odor transfer (`quranic:root_000609:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ); 78:37-38 `ر ح م` (ٱلرَّحْمَٰنِ/ٱلرَّحْمَٰنُ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا), `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ).
- Synthesis: Ritual covering differs from denial because its aim is removal and restored nearness, not concealment of what remains. Offering, mercy, restitution, and fragrance supply the positive outcome of purification.

#### Subchannel D. Remote Covered Place, Grave, and Return to Dust
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A person withdraws from inhabited space, enters a covered place or grave, and loses animated bodily form in return to earth.
- Active motifs: remote village, isolated tract, or grave (`quranic:root_001307:B012/m01`); covered mountain recess or low wall (`quranic:root_001307:B013/m01`); earth and grave-soil (`quranic:root_000178:B001/m01`); clinging and settling into the ground (`quranic:root_000025:B006/m01`); death expressed as final relief (`quranic:root_000609:B016/m01`); death or passing away (`quranic:root_001186:B002/m01`); return to an abode (`quranic:root_000065:B001/m01`); bad final condition (`quranic:root_001332:B006/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:31 `ف و ز` (مَفَازًا); 78:37 `ء ر ض` (ٱلْأَرْضِ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:39 `ء و ب` (مَـَٔابًا); 78:40 `ت ر ب` (تُرَٰبًۢا), `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ), `ك و ن` (كُنتُ).
- Synthesis: The wish for dust terminates in a concrete return sequence: withdrawal, covered place, grave-soil, cessation of spirit, and settled condition. It is the terminal form of covering because bodily distinction itself disappears.

### 33. P3 (78:31-40): A Wind-Driven Vessel Returning Under Command
- Semantic invariant: A voyage succeeds through command, route knowledge, wind, auxiliary craft, homecoming, and sufficient water stores.
- Surface relation: indirect; 78:37-39 supplies lordship, dominion, Spirit, chosen course, and return.
- Surprising reach: The cosmic court unexpectedly supports a complete maritime scene with chief pilot, leading guide, valley-route, wind, small service boat, arrival from travel, cistern, water skin, and provisions that “master the affair.”

#### Subchannel A. Chief Pilot, Guide, and Command Structure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A chief navigator directs subordinate movement, preserves the craft’s affairs, and exercises effective command over the voyage.
- Active motifs: chief of sailors or pilot (`quranic:root_000532:B017/m01`); leading guide followed by the herd or group (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); stewardship and maintenance (`quranic:root_001273:B004/m01`); sovereign power of command (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); effective ruling hand (`quranic:root_001693:B005/m01`); lordship and ownership (`quranic:root_000532:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: Navigation requires a hierarchy of practical command: pilot, guide, steward, and ruling hand. Authority is justified here by the coordinated preservation and direction of the moving whole.

#### Subchannel B. Wind, Auxiliary Boat, Route, and Homecoming
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Wind propels travel along a known route, a small auxiliary boat serves the larger voyage, and the traveler arrives back at the destination.
- Active motifs: moving wind and breeze (`quranic:root_000609:B003/m01`); small service boat accompanying ships (`quranic:root_001212:B011/m01`); centerline of road or valley (`quranic:root_001444:B006/m01`); arrival home from journey (`quranic:root_001207:B005/m01`); return to the destination (`quranic:root_000065:B001/m01`); approach from every direction (`quranic:root_000065:B004/m01`); choosing and adopting a course (`quranic:root_000018:B010/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ), `ء و ب` (مَـَٔابًا); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ), `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: The chosen return is rendered as navigated motion rather than an abstract destination. Wind, centerline, auxiliary boat, course adoption, and homecoming assign precise tools and stages to the voyage.

#### Subchannel C. Water Stores That Secure the Voyage
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Water is gathered, held, carried, and rationed so that the travelers retain control of their journey.
- Active motifs: water on which control of an expedition depends (`quranic:root_001444:B007/m01`); water skin (`quranic:root_001212:B009/m01`); basin retaining water for days (`quranic:root_000018:B006/m01`); abundant gathered water (`quranic:root_000532:B013/m01`); nighttime approach to a source (`quranic:root_001212:B008/m01`); gift or provision handed over (`quranic:root_001028:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: Water supply is the material condition of route autonomy. Basin, skin, source approach, and distributed provision explain how the crew “masters its affair” over distance.

### 34. P3 (78:31-40): Formation, Grapple, and Accurate Strike
- Semantic invariant: Conflict progresses from ordered formation through close contest to a directed strike whose success depends on grip, line, and target.
- Surface relation: indirect; 78:38 supplies the standing row and 78:40 the warning, forward action, hand, and visible outcome.
- Surprising reach: The courtly row can become a battle line with camp pavilion, vanguard, wrestling hold, rights contest, negotiated challenge, sword sheath, tool-handle, and penetrating thrust.

#### Subchannel A. Battle Line, Camp, and Vanguard
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Combatants assemble in a straight line, establish a field shelter, and position a forward element for engagement.
- Active motifs: aligned battle row (`quranic:root_000871:B001/m02`); resistance and combat (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`); military pavilion (`quranic:root_001186:B004/m01`); vanguard or front of an army (`quranic:root_001207:B007/m01`); public dominion and command (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); upright bodily readiness (`quranic:root_001273:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:31 `ف و ز` (مَفَازًا); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ), `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا); 78:40 `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ).
- Synthesis: The standing row becomes a prepared formation with command, shelter, and forward edge. Upright posture is the bodily readiness that lets courtly order transform into military order.

#### Subchannel B. Encirclement, Wrestling Hold, and Reciprocal Contest
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Opponents close distance, encircle or seize one another, assert competing claims, and struggle for advantage.
- Active motifs: wrestling or fighting grip (`quranic:root_000018:B011/m01`); encircling a target (`quranic:root_000300:B001/m01`); prevailing in reciprocal handling (`quranic:root_001028:B007/m01`); rival right-claims (`quranic:root_000347:B004/m01`); negotiated challenge (`quranic:root_001272:B009/m01`); prevailing in recompense (`quranic:root_000244:B005/m01`); resistance and match (`quranic:root_001273:B014/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:32 `ح د ق` (حَدَآئِقَ); 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ), `ع ط و` (عَطَآءً); 78:38, 78:40 `ق و ل` (قَالَ/يَقُولُ), `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ), `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ).
- Synthesis: Close conflict has a social and bodily structure: claim, negotiation, encirclement, seizure, resistance, and advantage. The grapple is the physical form of a dispute in which neither side initially yields.

#### Subchannel C. Warning, Weapon Grip, and Penetrating Target Line
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A lookout warns of danger, a combatant advances with a gripped weapon, and a straight thrust reaches its intended point.
- Active motifs: danger warning that awakens caution (`quranic:root_001488:B001/m01`); courageous advance (`quranic:root_001207:B006/m01`); sword or knife sheath (`quranic:root_001212:B010/m01`); handle or grip of an implement (`quranic:root_001693:B013/m01`); aiming and hitting a target (`quranic:root_000889:B003/m01`); penetrating straight thrust (`quranic:root_000347:B009/m01`); physical hand (`quranic:root_001693:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:38 `ص و ب` (صَوَابًۭا); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ن ذ ر` (أَنذَرْنَٰكُمْ), `ق د م` (قَدَّمَتْ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ), `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: The strike succeeds through a precise chain: warning produces readiness, hand grips, body advances, weapon leaves its sheath, and an undeviating line reaches the target.

## Standalone Subchannels

### S1. P1 (78:1-16): Arrow Manufacture, String Fit, and Non-Wounding Deflection
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A bow and arrow are measured, smoothed, strung, fitted at the nock, and tested by a shot that may deviate without cutting the target.
- Active motifs: measuring before cutting (`quranic:root_000434:B001/m01`); smooth straight surface (`quranic:root_000434:B008/m01`); bow clinging to its string (`quranic:root_000156:B005/m01`); arrow nock and string seat (`quranic:root_001188:B009/m01`); arrow deflecting without scratching (`quranic:root_001464:B005/m01`); visible mark for aim (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:2 `ن ب ء` (ٱلنَّبَإِ); 78:4-5 `ع ل م` (سَيَعْلَمُونَ); 78:8 `خ ل ق` (خَلَقْنَٰكُمْ); 78:12 `ب ن ي` (بَنَيْنَا), `ف و ق` (فَوْقَكُمْ).
- Synthesis: The branches form a compact craft-and-test scene: dimensions and finish precede stringing, the nock receives tension, and the shot’s line is judged by whether it reaches or glances away from the marked target.

### S2. P2 (78:17-30): Deictic Question, Temporal Reference, and Clause Segmentation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A discourse identifies which entity or time is at issue, addresses an interlocutor, explains the intended referent, and separates the resulting clause into intelligible units.
- Active motifs: interrogative selection “which?” (`quranic:root_000074:B004/m01`); pronoun support element (`quranic:root_000074:B005/m01`); temporal interrogative “when?” (`quranic:root_000074:B006/m01`); expression of large number (`quranic:root_000074:B007/m01`); near or distant vocative (`quranic:root_000074:B008/m01`); explanatory particle (`quranic:root_000074:B009/m01`); affirmative oath opening (`quranic:root_000074:B010/m01`); grammatical separator (`quranic:root_001159:B015/m01`); joining written units (`quranic:root_001283:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:17 `ف ص ل` (ٱلْفَصْلِ); 78:28 `ء ي ي` (بِـَٔايَٰتِنَا); 78:29 `ك ت ب` (كِتَٰبًۭا).
- Synthesis: The scene is a discourse-control system rather than a list of particles: referent, time, quantity, addressee, explanation, assertion, and boundary cooperate to make a clause identifiable and complete.

### S3. P2 (78:17-30): Waterskin Sealing and Journey Provision
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A traveler prepares a leather vessel by wetting and sealing its seams, secures it with straps, fills it, and adds it to the journey stores.
- Active motifs: wetting a skin so its seams swell shut (`quranic:root_000691:B004/m01`); seasoning and sealing a water skin (`quranic:root_000783:B013/m01`); securing belly-rope or strap (`quranic:root_000345:B002/m01`); leather strap (`quranic:root_000769:B003/m01`); stored food and water for travel (`quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`); drinking vessel or place (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:20 `س ي ر` (سُيِّرَتِ), `س ر ب` (سَرَابًا); 78:23 `ح ق ب` (أَحْقَابًۭا); 78:24 `ش ر ب` (شَرَابًا); 78:30 `ز ي د` (نَّزِيدَكُمْ).
- Synthesis: Wetting, swelling, sealing, strapping, filling, and storing create one complete preparation sequence. The vessel’s integrity is a prerequisite for portable refreshment.

### S4. P3 (78:31-40): Wound, Liability, and Required Compensation
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A bodily injury is inflicted and observed, liability attaches to the responsible party, and a legally required payment settles the wound.
- Active motifs: bodily wound or gash (`quranic:root_001316:B003/m01`); wound carrying mandatory compensation (`quranic:root_001488:B003/m01`); holding a person accountable for an offense (`quranic:root_000018:B002/m01`); illness or condition taking the body (`quranic:root_000018:B007/m01`); fixed private right (`quranic:root_000347:B003/m01`); debt recovery (`quranic:root_000244:B003/m01`); bodily pain lodged in an organ (`quranic:root_001273:B019/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36 `ج ز ي` (جَزَآءًۭ); 78:38 `ك ل م` (يَتَكَلَّمُونَ), `ق و م` (يَقُومُ); 78:39 `ء خ ذ` (ٱتَّخَذَ), `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ن ذ ر` (أَنذَرْنَٰكُمْ).
- Synthesis: Injury becomes a legal process with wound, sufferer, responsible agent, established right, and collected payment. The compensation branch prevents bodily harm from remaining outside the pericope’s wider settlement logic.

### S5. P3 (78:31-40): Managed Herd from Maturity to Milking and Movement
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A herd is organized under a leader, selected animals reach useful maturity, females are milked, and trained mounts move in controlled gaits.
- Active motifs: herd of wild cattle or camels (`quranic:root_000532:B014/m01`); leading animal followed by the rest (`quranic:root_001444:B008/m01`); animal mature enough for load or breeding (`quranic:root_000347:B008/m01`); full strength and condition of livestock (`quranic:root_000347:B013/m01`); female gathering multiple milk streams (`quranic:root_000871:B002/m01`); honored prepared riding animals (`quranic:root_001212:B013/m01`); controlled horse gait (`quranic:root_001212:B014/m01`); docile camel (`quranic:root_000178:B009/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:33, 78:40 `ت ر ب` (أَتْرَابًۭا/تُرَٰبًۢا); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا); 78:39 `ح ق ق` (ٱلْحَقُّ); 78:40 `ق ر ب` (قَرِيبًا).
- Synthesis: The livestock scene is operationally complete: leadership orders the herd, maturity determines use, milking extracts provision, and gait training governs movement. Docility is the relational state that permits these operations.

### S6. Whole-Surah (78:1-40): Alternating Well Drawers and Measured Water Shares
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: A team takes turns at a well, using an upright pulley and a balanced bucket handle to lift water, pour it into a drinking place, and distribute known shares.
