- Active motifs: alternating water drawers serving people or livestock (`quranic:root_000433:B011/m01`); bucket or water-skin handle that balances the load (`quranic:root_000741:B008/m01`); upright pulley apparatus at a well (`quranic:root_001273:B012/m01`); allotted water share (`quranic:root_000783:B002/m01`); drinking place or vessel (`quranic:root_000783:B004/m01`); pouring water into a trough or receptacle (`quranic:root_001458:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:3 `خ ل ف` (مُخْتَلِفُونَ); 78:14 `م و ه` (مَآءًۭ); 78:24 `ش ر ب` (شَرَابًا); 78:35 `س م ع` (يَسْمَعُونَ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ).
- Synthesis: The apparatus materializes the rain-to-growth reading by placing coordinated labor between descended water and usable provision. Alternating drawers, a standing mechanism, and fixed shares also reframe the Surah's measured cycles and distributions as a physical system in which access depends on ordered turns rather than unregulated abundance.

### S7. Whole-Surah (78:1-40): Drying and Packing Road Rations
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Meat is cut into a travel portion, arranged under sun or over embers, combined with a compact lump of fat, packed into a two-pouched bag, and carried toward a return-place.
- Active motifs: specific strip of camel flesh (`quranic:root_001365:B010/m01`); meat laid out in the sun or over embers and carried on a journey (`quranic:root_000871:B003/m01`); solid lump of ghee (`quranic:root_001304:B006/m01`); two-pouched traveling bag (`quranic:root_000400:B009/m01`); stored food or water for travel (`quranic:root_000657:B005/m01`); return to an abode (`quranic:root_000065:B001/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:15 `خ ر ج` (لِّنُخْرِجَ); 78:16 `ل ف ف` (أَلْفَافًا); 78:22, 78:39 `ء و ب` (مَـَٔابًۭا); 78:30 `ز ي د` (نَّزِيدَكُمْ); 78:33 `ك ع ب` (كَوَاعِبَ); 78:38 `ص ف ف` (صَفًّۭا).
- Synthesis: Portable food materializes the Surah's route-and-return imagery as a bodily journey requiring durable provision. It also sharpens the destination contrast: prepared nourishment sustains transit, while the punitive destination denies refreshment and the garden destination culminates in a filled cup.

### S8. Whole-Surah (78:1-40): Concealed-Hand Game and Forced Disclosure
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: One player holds an unseen item in the hand, another calls for what is held to be produced, and the possession is exposed and taken out.
- Active motifs: game requiring what is in the hand to be brought out (`quranic:root_000400:B010/m01`); asking and requesting (`quranic:root_000661:B001/m01`); physical hand (`quranic:root_001693:B001/m01`); taking an object by hand (`quranic:root_001028:B001/m01`); extracting a concealed item in the game (`quranic:root_000400:B002/m03`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:1 `س ء ل` (يَتَسَآءَلُونَ); 78:15 `خ ر ج` (لِّنُخْرِجَ); 78:36 `ع ط و` (عَطَآءًۭ); 78:40 `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: The game materializes the Surah's disclosure arc: questioning concerns a concealed content, and resolution requires that content to be brought into view. The final vision of what one's hands sent ahead intensifies the resonance, because the hand is no longer merely the hiding place but the exposed bearer of the deed.

### S9. Whole-Surah (78:1-40): Marked Gaming Lots and the Allocation of Shares
- Reading type: latent/lexical
- Scene or process: Smooth marked shafts are gathered in a leather container, a lot emerges for its owner, and the result assigns or divides a share among participants.
- Active motifs: leather container holding gaming arrows or lots (`quranic:root_000532:B010/m01`); smooth gaming shaft (`quranic:root_000434:B008/m01`); distinguishing mark placed on a lot (`quranic:root_001040:B002/m02`); winning lot emerging for its owner (`quranic:root_001186:B001/m02`); division of a share among partners (`quranic:root_000400:B012/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:4-5 `ع ل م` (سَيَعْلَمُونَ); 78:8 `خ ل ق` (خَلَقْنَٰكُمْ); 78:15 `خ ر ج` (لِّنُخْرِجَ); 78:31 `ف و ز` (مَفَازًا); 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ).
- Synthesis: The lot scene usefully pressures the primary judgment reading by supplying a concrete allocation mechanism governed by chance. Against it, the Surah's exhaustive account, proportionate recompense, and measured grant define judgment as the opposite of an arbitrary draw: the assigned outcome is known and calibrated rather than accidentally won.

### S10. Whole-Surah (78:1-40): Coronation, Pledged Submission, and Licensed Audience
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A ruler is crowned, a subject bows with hand at the chest, gives the hand as a pledge, enters a covenant of protection, and waits for authorization to approach or speak.
- Active motifs: crown covering and investing a king (`quranic:root_001307:B015/m01`); bowed head or hand-on-chest submission (`quranic:root_001307:B014/m01`); surrendered hand given as pledge or guarantee (`quranic:root_001693:B006/m01`); covenant and protected relation (`quranic:root_000532:B011/m01`); kingship and sovereign authority (`quranic:root_001444:B003/m01`); chamberlain's permission to enter or speak (`quranic:root_000022:B004/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:36-37, 78:39 `ر ب ب` (رَّبِّكَ/رَّبِّ/رَبِّهِۦ); 78:37-38 `م ل ك` (يَمْلِكُونَ/ٱلْمَلَٰٓئِكَةُ); 78:38 `ء ذ ن` (أَذِنَ); 78:40 `ك ف ر` (ٱلْكَافِرُ), `ي د ي` (يَدَاهُ).
- Synthesis: The ceremony materializes the sovereign court of 78:37-38 through crown, posture, pledged hand, covenant, and controlled audience. Restricted speech is thereby reframed as embodied political order: authority is visibly invested, submission is enacted before it is verbalized, and address occurs only within an authorized relation.

### S11. Whole-Surah (78:1-40): Sabbath Observance as Instituted Cessation
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: A community enters a named day, suspends ordinary labor, treats the interval as a festival, and distinguishes it from the recurring work by which livelihood is maintained.
- Active motifs: Sabbath day observed as a festival with work suspended (`quranic:root_000665:B003/m01`); cutting and removal (`quranic:root_000665:B002/m01`); livelihood sustained by food, drink, and means of subsistence (`quranic:root_001067:B002/m01`); alternation between workers or tasks (`quranic:root_000609:B008/m01`); night adjoining and delimiting a day (`quranic:root_001392:B003/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:9 `س ب ت` (سُبَاتًۭا); 78:10 `ل ي ل` (ٱلْيْلَ); 78:11 `ع ي ش` (مَعَاشًۭا); 78:38 `ر و ح` (ٱلرُّوحُ).
- Synthesis: Sabbath observance reframes the primary sleep-and-rest reading as more than biological recovery: cessation can be communally instituted, calendrically bounded, and set against livelihood-producing labor. The rite therefore strengthens the Surah's alternation between rest and work while giving sabat a concrete social form.

### S12. P1-P2-P3 Causal-Sequence and Role-Progression Bridge: Work Becomes Assessed Payment
- Reading type: mixed
- Scene or process: P1 fixes compensation for work and identifies a known yield or levy; P2 advances the payer into the role of one who renders the due amount; P3 weighs the payment, appraises its value, and brings it into an active market.
- Active motifs: wage or fee agreed for work (`quranic:root_000248:B005/m01`); money, yield, tax, or duty paid on a known basis (`quranic:root_000400:B003/m01`); bringing and handing over a thing (`quranic:root_000009:B002/m01`); known ounce weight for dirhams or oil (`quranic:root_001677:B004/m01`); valuation and pricing of goods (`quranic:root_001273:B010/m01`); market entering active circulation (`quranic:root_001273:B018/m01`).
- Ayah anchors: 78:6, 78:9-11, 78:13 `ج ع ل` (نَجْعَلِ/جَعَلْنَا); 78:15 `خ ر ج` (لِّنُخْرِجَ); 78:18 `ء ت ي` (فَتَأْتُونَ); 78:31 `و ق ي` (لِلْمُتَّقِينَ); 78:38 `ق و م` (يَقُومُ).
- Synthesis: The causal bridge carries P1's productive order into P2's rendering of an obligation and P3's measured circulation of value. Wage, levy, delivery, weight, and appraisal materialize proportional recompense as a completed economic sequence: value originates in work, becomes due, is rendered, and enters recognized exchange only after measure and price are established.
